# Read, Recall, Colour: full audit, critique and fix plan

Date: 2026-09-09. Method: `$impeccable audit` then `$impeccable critique` over the whole drill: the door (library and section pages), the briefing page, the runner, the handover and marking wait, and the Report Card. Four surfaces, one journey. Evidence: source read in full (about 9,000 lines), the dev server at localhost driven in the in-app browser (lab at `/activity-lab`, harnesses at `/src/dev/section-launch-preview.html` and `/src/dev/report-card-preview.html`), contrast computed from rendered colours through a canvas, and the impeccable 4.1.0 detector on files and rendered URLs. Related: [[RRC-BRIEFING-CRITIQUE-2026-09-08]], [[COPY-CORRECTIONS]], [[ASSIGN-AN-ACTIVITY]].

What was not possible: the browser pane returned black frames for scrolled or emulated screenshots, so below-the-fold and phone checks are DOM-measured rather than eyeballed. The briefing page has no harness and was audited from code. The overlay mode of the detector does not exist in 4.1.0 (`impeccable live` is a variant tool needing a config file), so the rendered-URL scan replaced it. The first-run walkthrough card and the phone "Next stimulus" button are not rendered by the lab.

## Part 1: Audit (technical)

### Scores by surface

| Surface | A11y | Perf | Responsive | Theming | Anti-patterns | Total /20 | Band |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Door and briefing | 2 | 2 | 3 | 2 | 2 | 11 | Acceptable |
| Runner (stage, ready beat, pause, leave, finish) | 2 | 3 | 3 | 2 | 4 | 14 | Good |
| Marking wait and Report Card | 2 | 3 | 3 | 3 | 3 | 14 | Good |
| **Journey** | **2** | **3** | **3** | **2** | **3** | **13** | **Acceptable** |

Issue counts across the journey: P0 3, P1 16, P2 24, P3 15. The three P0s are copy and flow, not pixels, and they are in Part 2.

### Anti-patterns verdict, per surface

- **Door:** not first-order slop (nobody guesses "LSAT drill, lavender dusk over a meadow"), but caught by the second-order reflex: the editorial-serif, mono-label, pastel-sky, floating-demo-card lane is the saturated one. Countable tells: two identical card grids (four step cards at `section-launch.css:772-830`, three comparison cards at `:1206-1253`), traffic-light window dots on the demo card (`:362-380`), a glass chip with `backdrop-filter` (`:211-225`), a hero-metric stat strip (`:1436-1482`, mitigated by honest data), hover lifts with shadows on three controls (`:286`, `:795`, `:1426`), a drenched gradient closing panel (`:1575`), and the chip restating the H1 verbatim (`SectionLaunchFrame.tsx:279-283`). Also, the door is built on cream, the colour the same owner vetoed for the Report Card as "overdone and too AI looking". That is a brief-level contradiction.
- **Runner:** clean. A deliberate LawHub mirror; familiarity is the feature. The only tells are under the surface: 13 raw slate hex literals in `section.css` and a Helvetica Neue stack that renders as Arial on the Windows laptops students use.
- **Report Card:** pass with two hits. `border-l-2 border-[var(--rc-signal)]` on the next-step sentence (`SectionReportCard.tsx:511`) is the banned side stripe, in the colour the brief reserves for sound. The console header (52px band, stat sentence, three figures) is the hero-metric shape, saved only by the band leading and each figure carrying a sentence. Four nested bordered boxes inside the card (`:318`, `:742`, `:987`, `:1004`).

### P1 findings (fix before the next student runs one)

**Accessibility, measured in the browser**

1. Runner focus ring is `rgb(29 29 31 / 0.35)`: 2.17:1 on white, 2.15:1 on the footer, 1.00:1 on the indigo field where the walkthrough's Hide button sits. `section.css:243`. WCAG 1.4.11 needs 3:1.
2. Runner Pause and Leave are 62x30 and 59x29 on desktop, 54x30 and 51x29 on a phone, and the phone rule shrinks their padding further. `section.css:73-109`, `:262-270`. PRODUCT.md binds 44x44 by being that size.
3. Runner ready-beat countdown "Starting in 3" is `#94a3b8` on white, 2.56:1 at 13px. `section.css:523`.
4. Runner Pause button border `#cbd5e1` on white is 1.48:1, the control's only boundary.
5. Leave dialog has `aria-modal` but no focus trap and no Escape: three Tabs land on the page behind it; Escape leaves it open. `SectionStage.tsx:275-300`.
6. Runner has zero headings; "Stimulus 1 of 23" is a span. Report Card has zero headings and unnamed sections; the 52px band label is a `p`. `SectionReportCard.tsx:489-497`.
7. Door demo play button is 28x28 and its focus ring is white on paper, 1.05:1, and it is the first Tab stop on the page. `RecallDemo.tsx:170-178`, `section-launch.css:330, :423`.
8. Door crumb "Library" (the exit) is 55x17. Drawer tabs are 43px. `section-launch.css:185-197`, `:1063-1077`.
9. Door demo step label carries `aria-live="polite"` inside a loop that cycles every 1.5 to 4.2 s for as long as the page is open. `RecallDemo.tsx:163-165`.
10. Door heading outline is H1, H3, H2 (H1, six H4, H2 with the drawer open). `SectionLaunchFrame.tsx:346, 429, 455, 502`.
11. Report Card ticks 30x45 at 1180 and 29x45 at 375; transport 34px; Hear it, Previous, Next 26px; summaries 16px; dispute link 16px. `report-card.css:119-131, :229, :356`; `SectionReportCard.tsx:743, :804, :1185`.
12. Run-history band inks fail AA: 3.79:1 and 3.85:1 on the lab ground, 4.24 and 4.31 on the Workroom page, and the band is colour-only. `SectionRunHistory.tsx:48-53`.
13. Handover subtitle `#64748b` on the warmed ground is 4.17:1. `section.css:645`.
14. Ledger ticks expose only "Stimulus N"; band, recital and ungraded state are invisible to a screen reader. `SectionReportCard.tsx:659-673`.

**Correctness**

15. Focus-set chips are off by one: `(s.item_order ?? i) + 1` on a 1-based `item_order`, so "Stimulus 5" names stimulus 4 everywhere else on the card. `SectionReportCard.tsx:1257`. Unrendered by harness and lab.
16. Upload-failed flow: when the recording fails to upload but the rows saved, `onComplete` fires at once, so the sentence "Your recording did not reach us, open this section again from the same browser" is replaced within a frame by "Marking your run", and three minutes later by "Still being marked, it will be here when you come back", which is false until she reopens the section in that browser. `SectionRunner.tsx:1030-1040`, `SectionReport.tsx:86-89`.

**Performance (door)**

17. 191 to 196 animations run at rest, 170 of them per-path transforms on grass blades inside one `will-change` layer, plus the demo's `setInterval` and per-frame typewriter, none gated on visibility. `section-launch.css:147-157`, `MeadowScene.tsx:203-216`. A scroll-interpolated `blur(14px)` on the 96px headline layer. `SectionLaunchFrame.tsx:181, :277`.

### P2 findings (next pass)

- Runner: `section.css` has 13 distinct slate hex literals beside its 7 tokens; `.ac-section` is not recorded as a sanctioned world in DESIGN.md or the surface brief, and DESIGN.md's question lock says Outfit while the runner mirrors LawHub's Fraunces. Record or code needs a ruling. `.is-finishing` copies `hsl(38 36% 93%)` by value.
- Runner: the goal is never on screen. The CSS for a lighter goal beside the clock (`section.css:283-286`) is dead; the stage only renders elapsed. She cannot pace against a target she cannot see.
- Runner: the ready beat says "Say something to check your microphone" but lasts 3 s and does not wait for a level; dead input is reported only after two silent windows of 8 s or more.
- Runner: `kbd space` hint still renders on a coarse pointer beside the on-screen button.
- Runner: the walkthrough's coach-step labels are 0.66 and 0.68rem, under the 11px floor.

   107	        <i key={i} style={{ animationDelay: `${(i * 0.09).toFixed(2)}s` }} />
   108	      ))}
   109	    </span>
   110	  );
   111	}
   112	
   113	function clock(seconds: number): string {
   114	  const m = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
   115	  const s = seconds % 60;
   116	  return `${m}:${String(s).padStart(2, "0")}`;
   117	}
   118	
   119	export function RecallDemo() {
   120	  const reduced = useReducedMotion();
   121	  const [idx, setIdx] = useState(0);
   122	  const [playing, setPlaying] = useState(true);
   123	  const [seconds, setSeconds] = useState(0);
   124	  const timer = useRef<number | null>(null);
   125	
   126	  const running = playing && !reduced;
   127	
   128	  useEffect(() => {
   129	    if (!running) return;
   130	    timer.current = window.setTimeout(() => setIdx((i) => (i + 1) % BEATS.length), BEATS[idx].ms);
   131	    return () => {
   132	      if (timer.current) window.clearTimeout(timer.current);
   133	    };
   134	  }, [idx, running]);
   135	
   136	  useEffect(() => {
   137	    if (!running) return;
   138	    const id = window.setInterval(() => setSeconds((s) => (s + 1) % 1500), 1000);
   139	    return () => window.clearInterval(id);
   140	  }, [running]);
   141	
   142	  // Reduced motion: the finished state, held, with nothing moving.
   143	  const beat = reduced ? BEATS[BEATS.length - 1] : BEATS[idx];
   144	  const phase = beat.kind;
   145	  const sentence = beat.i;
   146	  const gone = phase === "recall" || phase === "tally" || phase === "hold";
   147	  const spacePressed = phase === "space";
   148	
   149	  const tellText = useTypewriter(TELL[Math.min(sentence, 2)], phase === "tell", 2000, Boolean(reduced));
   150	  const recallText = useTypewriter(RECALL, phase === "recall", 3600, Boolean(reduced));
   151	  const showTally = phase === "tally" || phase === "hold";
   152	  const carried = DETAILS.filter((d) => d.carried).length;
   153	
   154	  return (
   155	    <div className="rd-demo" data-phase={phase}>
   156	      <div className="rd-demo-bar">
   157	        <span className="rd-demo-dots" aria-hidden="true">
   158	          <i />
   159	          <i />
   160	          <i />
   161	        </span>
   162	        <span className="rd-demo-title">Read, Recall, Colour</span>
   163	        <span className="rd-demo-step" aria-live="polite">
   164	          {STEP_LABEL[phase]}
   165	        </span>
   166	        <span className="rd-demo-rec">
   167	          <i className="rd-rec-dot" data-live={running || undefined} aria-hidden="true" />
   168	          <span className="rd-demo-clock">{clock(seconds)}</span>
   169	        </span>
   170	        <button
   171	          type="button"
   172	          className="rd-demo-play"
   173	          onClick={() => setPlaying((p) => !p)}
   174	          aria-label={playing ? "Pause the demonstration" : "Play the demonstration"}
   175	          disabled={Boolean(reduced)}
   176	        >
   177	          {playing && !reduced ? <Pause size={13} strokeWidth={2.4} /> : <Play size={13} strokeWidth={2.4} />}
   178	        </button>
   179	      </div>
   180	
   181	      <div className="rd-demo-body">
   182	        <div className="rd-paper" data-gone={gone || undefined}>
   183	          <span className="rd-demo-label">Stimulus</span>
   184	          <p className="rd-stim">
   185	            {STIMULUS.map((s, i) => (
   186	              <span
   187	                key={i}
   188	                className="rd-stim-line"
   189	                data-active={!gone && i === sentence ? "true" : undefined}
   190	                data-done={!gone && i < sentence ? "true" : undefined}
   191	              >
   192	                {s}{" "}
   193	              </span>
   194	            ))}
   195	          </p>
   196	          <AnimatePresence>
   197	            {gone && (
   198	              <motion.p
   199	                className="rd-gone"
   200	                initial={reduced ? false : { opacity: 0, y: 6 }}
   201	                animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
   202	                exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
   203	                transition={{ duration: 0.5, delay: 0.5, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] }}
   204	              >
   205	                The stimulus is gone. Say the whole argument back from memory.
   206	              </motion.p>
   207	            )}
   208	          </AnimatePresence>
   209	          <div className="rd-keyrow" aria-hidden="true">
   210	            <span className="rd-key" data-pressed={spacePressed || undefined}>
   211	              space
   212	            </span>
   213	            <span className="rd-keyhint">{gone ? "once more to move on" : "when you finish the piece"}</span>
   214	          </div>
   215	        </div>
   216	
   217	        <div className="rd-voice">
   218	          <span className="rd-demo-label">Out loud</span>
   219	          <div className="rd-voice-stack">
   220	            <AnimatePresence mode="popLayout" initial={false}>
   221	              {!gone && (phase === "tell" || phase === "colour") && (
   222	                <motion.div
   223	                  key={`tell-${sentence}`}
   224	                  className="rd-bubble"
   225	                  layout
   226	                  initial={reduced ? false : { opacity: 0, y: 14, scale: 0.98 }}
   227	                  animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0, scale: 1 }}
   228	                  exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -10, transition: { duration: 0.25 } }}
   229	                  transition={{ duration: 0.45, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] }}
   230	                >
   231	                  <span className="rd-bubble-head">
   232	                    <Mic size={12} strokeWidth={2.4} aria-hidden="true" />
   233	                    You say
   234	                    <Waveform live={phase === "tell"} />
   235	                  </span>
   236	                  <p>
   237	                    “{tellText}
   238	                    {phase === "tell" && <span className="rd-caret" aria-hidden="true" />}”
   239	                  </p>
   240	                </motion.div>
   241	              )}
   242	              {!gone && phase === "colour" && (
   243	                <motion.div
   244	                  key={`colour-${sentence}`}
   245	                  className="rd-colour"
   246	                  layout
   247	                  initial={reduced ? false : { opacity: 0, scale: 0.9, rotate: -1.5 }}
   248	                  animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1, rotate: 0 }}
   249	                  exit={{ opacity: 0, transition: { duration: 0.2 } }}
   250	                  transition={{ type: "spring", stiffness: 380, damping: 26 }}
   251	                >
   252	                  <span className="rd-colour-head">Colour it</span>
   253	                  <p>“{COLOUR[sentence]}”</p>
   254	                </motion.div>
   255	              )}
   256	              {gone && (
   257	                <motion.div
   258	                  key="recall"
   259	                  className="rd-bubble rd-bubble-recall"
   260	                  layout
   261	                  initial={reduced ? false : { opacity: 0, y: 14 }}
   262	                  animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
   263	                  exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
   264	                  transition={{ duration: 0.45, delay: 0.35, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] }}
   265	                >
   266	                  <span className="rd-bubble-head">
   267	                    <Mic size={12} strokeWidth={2.4} aria-hidden="true" />
   268	                    The Recall
   269	                    <Waveform live={phase === "recall"} />
   270	                  </span>
   271	                  <p>
   272	                    “{recallText}
   273	                    {phase === "recall" && <span className="rd-caret" aria-hidden="true" />}”
   274	                  </p>
   275	                </motion.div>
   276	              )}
   277	              {showTally && (
   278	                <motion.div
   279	                  key="tally"
   280	                  className="rd-tally"
   281	                  layout
   282	                  initial={reduced ? false : { opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
   283	                  animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
   284	                  exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
   285	                  transition={{ duration: 0.4, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] }}

     1	import { useMemo } from "react";
     2	import { motion, useReducedMotion, useTransform, type MotionValue } from "framer-motion";
     3	
     4	/**
     5	 * The dusk meadow behind the Read, Recall, Colour door.
     6	 *
     7	 * Painted, not photographed, and painted at render time: every ridge is a
     8	 * seeded sum of sines, every blade of grass a seeded quadratic, so the scene
     9	 * ships as geometry rather than as three PNGs that have to arrive. It is one
    10	 * picture cut into five depth planes -- sky, far hills, mid hills, near hills,
    11	 * foreground grass -- and each plane is its own layer so the door can move them
    12	 * at different rates as she scrolls. The demo card sits BETWEEN the near hills
    13	 * and the grass, which is what makes the grass pass in front of it.
    14	 *
    15	 * Palette is the runner's own indigo field lifted into a sky, so the door and
    16	 * the drill room read as one evening.
    17	 */
    18	
    19	const W = 1600;
    20	const H = 900;
    21	
    22	function mulberry32(seed: number) {
    23	  let t = seed;
    24	  return () => {
    25	    t += 0x6d2b79f5;
    26	    let r = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), 1 | t);
    27	    r ^= r + Math.imul(r ^ (r >>> 7), 61 | r);
    28	    return ((r ^ (r >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
    29	  };
    30	}
    31	
    32	/** A smooth ridge: a few sines summed, closed to the bottom edge. */
    33	function ridge(seed: number, base: number, amp: number): string {
    34	  const rng = mulberry32(seed);
    35	  const waves = Array.from({ length: 4 }, (_, i) => ({
    36	    f: (0.6 + rng() * 1.4) * (i + 1) * 0.0018,
    37	    p: rng() * Math.PI * 2,
    38	    a: amp / (i + 1.2),
    39	  }));
    40	  const pts: string[] = [];
    41	  for (let x = -40; x <= W + 40; x += 16) {
    42	    const y = waves.reduce((s, w) => s + w.a * Math.sin(w.f * x + w.p), base);
    43	    pts.push(`${x.toFixed(1)} ${y.toFixed(1)}`);
    44	  }
    45	  // Closed 700 units below the frame, not at its edge: the plane rises as she
    46	  // scrolls, and the skirt is what keeps the ground beneath it painted.
    47	  return `M -40 ${H + 700} L ${pts.join(" L ")} L ${W + 40} ${H + 700} Z`;
    48	}
    49	
    50	interface Blade {
    51	  d: string;
    52	  w: number;
    53	  c: string;
    54	  delay: number;
    55	  x: number;
    56	}
    57	interface Flower {
    58	  x: number;
    59	  y: number;
    60	  r: number;
    61	  c: string;
    62	}
    63	
    64	const GREENS = ["#4f6d55", "#5f7d64", "#6f8f74", "#7d9a80", "#8fa896"];
    65	const FLOWERS = ["#f2b544", "#f7d27a", "#f6f3ea", "#c9c3ec", "#f3c9a0"];
    66	
    67	function grass(seed: number, count: number): { blades: Blade[]; flowers: Flower[] } {
    68	  const rng = mulberry32(seed);
    69	  const blades: Blade[] = [];
    70	  const flowers: Flower[] = [];
    71	  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    72	    const x = -20 + rng() * (W + 40);
    73	    const h = 36 + rng() * 96;
    74	    const lean = (rng() - 0.5) * 60;
    75	    const y0 = H + 10;
    76	    blades.push({
    77	      x,
    78	      d: `M ${x.toFixed(1)} ${y0} Q ${(x + lean * 0.35).toFixed(1)} ${(y0 - h * 0.62).toFixed(1)} ${(x + lean).toFixed(1)} ${(y0 - h).toFixed(1)}`,
    79	      w: 2.5 + rng() * 4,
    80	      c: GREENS[Math.floor(rng() * GREENS.length)],
    81	      delay: rng() * 4,
    82	    });
    83	    if (rng() < 0.22) {
    84	      flowers.push({
    85	        x: x + lean,
    86	        y: y0 - h - 3,
    87	        r: 3 + rng() * 4.5,
    88	        c: FLOWERS[Math.floor(rng() * FLOWERS.length)],
    89	      });
    90	    }
    91	  }
    92	  return { blades, flowers };
    93	}
    94	
    95	export interface MeadowSceneProps {
    96	  /** 0 at the top of the hero, 1 when the hero has scrolled fully past. */
    97	  progress: MotionValue<number>;
    98	  /** Content drawn between the near hills and the foreground grass. */
    99	  children?: React.ReactNode;
   100	}

   108	
   109	export function MeadowScene({ progress, children }: MeadowSceneProps) {
   110	  const reduced = useReducedMotion();
   111	
   112	  const far = useMemo(() => ridge(11, 545, 42), []);

   130	  return (

   149	        <rect width={W} height={H} fill="url(#mw-sky-g)" />

   151	      </motion.svg>
   152

    11	  title: string;
    12	  subtitle: string | null;
    13	  instructions: ActivityInstructions;
    14	  /** Whether this page needs the breadcrumb line above its own heading. */
    15	  showBreadcrumbs?: boolean;
    16	  /** The right half of the crumb line: what this page is a page of. */
    17	  meta?: ReactNode;
    18	  /**
    19	   * The page's own primary action, if it has one.
    20	   *
    21	   * A section has one: Start. The library does not; its actions are the
    22	   * section rows, so its hero button carries her down to them instead.
    23	   */
    24	  primaryAction?: ReactNode;
    25	  /**
    26	   * The same action again at the foot of the page, worded for where it sits.
    27	   * A section passes a second Start here; the library needs nothing, since its
    28	   * default carries her back up to the sections.
    29	   */
    30	  closingAction?: ReactNode;
    31	  /** A caution under the hero, where there is one. It never disables anything. */
    32	  notice?: ReactNode;
    33	  /** Between the hero and the method. The four numbers, where there are four. */
    34	  strip?: ReactNode;
    35	  /** The slab below the method: her runs on a section, her sections in the library. */
    36	  children?: ReactNode;
    37	  /**
    38	   * Put the slab directly under the hero instead of at the foot of the page.
    39	   *
    40	   * The library's slab IS the point of the page — the sections she can start.
    41	   * Burying it under the editorial meant "Choose a section" was a long scroll
    42	   * away, and a student who did not scroll never found a way in.
    43	   */
    44	  sectionsFirst?: boolean;
    45	  onExit?: () => void;
    46	
    47	}
    48	
    49	const EASE = [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] as const;
    50	
    51	/** One authored entrance, shared by every block below the hero. */
    52	const reveal = {
    53	  initial: { opacity: 0, y: 28 },
    54	  whileInView: { opacity: 1, y: 0 },
    55	  viewport: { once: true, margin: "-12% 0px" },
    56	  transition: { duration: 0.8, ease: EASE },
    57	} as const;
    58	
    59	const STEP_KINDS = ["read", "tell", "colour", "recall"] as const;
    60	
    61	/**
    62	 * The little painted scene above each step.
    63	 *
    64	 * Four small dioramas drawn in the door's own vocabulary: a page, a voice, a
    65	 * stroke of colour, a page emptying. They move while the step is in view and
    66	 * stop under reduced motion; none of them carries anything the words do not.
    67	 */
    68	function StepScene({ kind }: { kind: (typeof STEP_KINDS)[number] }) {
    69	  if (kind === "read") {
    70	    return (
    71	      <div className="rd-scene rd-sc-read" aria-hidden="true">
    72	        <div className="rd-sc-page">
    73	          <i className="rd-sc-line" style={{ width: "88%" }} />
    74	          <i className="rd-sc-line" style={{ width: "72%" }} />
    75	          <i className="rd-sc-line" style={{ width: "94%" }} />
    76	          <i className="rd-sc-line" style={{ width: "58%" }} />
    77	          <span className="rd-sc-sweep" />
    78	        </div>
    79	      </div>
    80	    );
    81	  }
    82	  if (kind === "tell") {
    83	    return (
    84	      <div className="rd-scene rd-sc-tell" aria-hidden="true">
    85	        <div className="rd-sc-bubble">
    86	          <Mic size={13} strokeWidth={2.4} />
    87	          <span className="rd-wave" data-live="true">
    88	            {Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, i) => (
    89	              <i key={i} style={{ animationDelay: `${(i * 0.09).toFixed(2)}s` }} />
    90	            ))}
    91	          </span>
    92	          <span className="rd-sc-bubble-text">in your own words</span>
    93	        </div>
    94	        <div className="rd-sc-page rd-sc-page-turned">
    95	          <i className="rd-sc-line" style={{ width: "80%" }} />
    96	          <i className="rd-sc-line" style={{ width: "64%" }} />
    97	        </div>
    98	      </div>
    99	    );
   100	  }
   101	  if (kind === "colour") {
   102	    return (
   103	      <div className="rd-scene rd-sc-colour" aria-hidden="true">
   104	        <span className="rd-sc-stroke" />
   105	        <div className="rd-sc-chip">
   106	          <span className="rd-sc-chip-head">Colour it</span>
   107	          “Who counted that?”
   108	        </div>
   109	      </div>
   110	    );
   111	  }
   112	  return (
   113	    <div className="rd-scene rd-sc-recall" aria-hidden="true">
   114	      <div className="rd-sc-page rd-sc-page-fading">
   115	        <i className="rd-sc-line" style={{ width: "88%" }} />
   116	        <i className="rd-sc-line" style={{ width: "72%" }} />
   117	        <i className="rd-sc-line" style={{ width: "94%" }} />
   118	        <i className="rd-sc-line" style={{ width: "58%" }} />
   119	      </div>
   120	      <span className="rd-sc-key">space</span>
   121	    </div>
   122	  );
   123	}
   124	
   125	/**
   126	 * The door to Read, Recall, Colour, drawn once for both of its pages.
   127	 *
   128	 * There are two doors and one design. The library is where she picks tonight's
   129	 * section; the section page is where she reads its clock and starts it. They
   130	 * share the hero, the demonstration, the four steps and the briefing, and
   131	 * differ only in the slab underneath.
   132	 *
   133	 * The page is built to be understood in one viewport: the method is performed
   134	 * in the hero before it is explained, the four steps are the next thing she
   135	 * meets, and the sections she can run sit directly beneath. The long briefing
   136	 * is one tab away and never in the way.
   137	 */
   138	export function SectionLaunchFrame({
   139	  title,
   140	  subtitle,
   141	  instructions,
   142	  showBreadcrumbs = true,
   143	  meta,
   144	  primaryAction,
   145	  closingAction,
   146	  notice,
   147	  strip,
   148	  children,
   149	  sectionsFirst = false,
   150	  onExit,
   151	
   152	}: SectionLaunchFrameProps) {
   153	  const reduced = useReducedMotion();
   154	
   155	  /**
   156	   * The four beats are `steps` inside ONE briefing section, not the sections.
   157	   * Only "How the method works" carries the procedure; rendering `sections`
   158	   * here would put the essay headings in the strip and lose the method.
   159	   */
   160	  const beatSection = useMemo(
   161	    () => instructions.briefing?.sections.find((s) => (s.steps?.length ?? 0) > 0),
   162	    [instructions.briefing],
   163	  );
   164	  const beats = beatSection?.steps ?? [];
   165	
   166	  const [beat, setBeat] = useState(0);
   167	  const [pane, setPane] = useState<"beat" | "briefing">("beat");
   168	
   169	  const heroRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
   170	  const drawerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
   171	  const sectionsRef = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);
   172	
   173	  /**
   174	   * The hero's scroll, 0 while it is fully on screen and 1 once it has left.
   175	   * The scene planes and the headline all ride this one number, so they move
   176	   * as one picture rather than as separate effects.
   177	   */
   178	  const { scrollYProgress } = useScroll({ target: heroRef, offset: ["start start", "end start"] });
   179	  const copyY = useTransform(scrollYProgress, [0, 1], [0, 160]);
   180	  const copyOpacity = useTransform(scrollYProgress, [0, 0.5], [1, 0]);
   181	  const copyFilter = useTransform(scrollYProgress, [0, 0.5], ["blur(0px)", "blur(14px)"]);
   182	  const copyScale = useTransform(scrollYProgress, [0, 0.6], [1, 0.94]);
   183	
   184	  const beatRefs = useRef<(HTMLButtonElement | null)[]>([]);
   185	  const onBeatKey = useCallback(
   186	    (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
   187	      const last = beats.length - 1;
   188	      let next: number | null = null;
   189	      if (e.key === "ArrowRight" || e.key === "ArrowDown") next = beat === last ? 0 : beat + 1;
   190	      if (e.key === "ArrowLeft" || e.key === "ArrowUp") next = beat === 0 ? last : beat - 1;
   191	      if (e.key === "Home") next = 0;
   192	      if (e.key === "End") next = last;
   193	      if (next === null) return;
   194	      e.preventDefault();
   195	      setBeat(next);
   196	      setPane("beat");
   197	      beatRefs.current[next]?.focus();
   198	    },
   199	    [beat, beats.length],
   200	  );
   201	
   202	  const paneRefs = useRef<(HTMLButtonElement | null)[]>([]);
   203	  const onPaneKey = useCallback(
   204	    (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
   205	      if (e.key !== "ArrowRight" && e.key !== "ArrowLeft") return;
   206	      e.preventDefault();
   207	      const next = pane === "beat" ? "briefing" : "beat";
   208	      setPane(next);
   209	      paneRefs.current[next === "beat" ? 0 : 1]?.focus();
   210	    },
   211	    [pane],
   212	  );
   213	
   214	  const openBriefing = useCallback(() => {
   215	    setPane("briefing");
   216	    drawerRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: reduced ? "auto" : "smooth", block: "start" });
   217	  }, [reduced]);
   218	
   219	  const goToSections = useCallback(() => {
   220	    const slab = sectionsRef.current;
   221	    if (!slab) return;
   222	    // The app can run inside an editor frame whose scrolling container swallows
   223	    // smooth document scrolling. Jump first, then focus the destination without
   224	    // starting a second browser-controlled scroll back toward the hero.
   225	    slab.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "auto", block: "start" });
   226	    slab.focus({ preventScroll: true });
   227	  }, []);
   228	
   229	
   230	  const briefing = instructions.briefing;
   231	  const current = beats[beat];
   232	
   233	  return (
   234	    <div className="sl">
   235	      {/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- hero */}
   236	      <section className="rd-hero" ref={heroRef} aria-labelledby="rd-title">
   237	        <div className="rd-hero-panel">
   238	          <MeadowScene progress={scrollYProgress}>
   239	            <RecallDemo />
   240	          </MeadowScene>
   241	
   242	          <div className="rd-hero-top">
   243	            <span className="rd-crumbs">
   244	              {showBreadcrumbs ? (
   245	                <>
   246	                  {onExit ? (
   247	                    <button type="button" className="sl-crumb-back" onClick={onExit}>
   248	                      Library
   249	                    </button>
   250	                  ) : (
   251	                    "Library"
   252	                  )}
   253	                  <span className="rd-crumb-sep">/</span>
   254	                  Out loud
   255	                  <span className="rd-crumb-sep">/</span>
   256	                  <strong>{title}</strong>
   257	                </>
   258	              ) : (
   259	                <>
   260	                  {onExit && (
   261	                    <>
   262	                      <button type="button" className="sl-crumb-back" onClick={onExit}>
   263	                        Library
   264	                      </button>
   265	                      <span className="rd-crumb-sep">/</span>
   266	                    </>
   267	                  )}
   268	                  <strong>Out loud</strong>
   269	                </>
   270	              )}
   271	            </span>
   272	            {meta && <span className="rd-meta">{meta}</span>}
   273	          </div>
   274	
   275	          <motion.div
   276	            className="rd-hero-copy"
   277	            style={reduced ? undefined : { y: copyY, opacity: copyOpacity, filter: copyFilter, scale: copyScale }}
   278	          >
   279	            <span className="sl-chip">
   280	              <Mic size={11} strokeWidth={2.6} aria-hidden="true" />
   281	              Read, Recall, Colour
   282	            </span>
   283	            <h1 className="sl-title" id="rd-title">
   284	              {/* "Read, Recall, Colour · PT102 Section 3" is one title in the
   285	                  record and two lines on the door: the drill, then the section
   286	                  it is run on, in the same italic the library gives "back".
   287	                  The library door's own title carries no " · ", so it renders
   288	                  whole. It used to hardcode "Recall Rephrase Colour" here, a
   289	                  name the product does not have, one line under a chip reading
   290	                  "Read, Recall, Colour". */}
   291	              {title.includes(" · ") ? (
   292	                <>
   293	                  {title.slice(0, title.indexOf(" · "))}
   294	                  <br />
   295	                  <em>{title.slice(title.indexOf(" · ") + 3)}</em>
   296	                </>
   297	              ) : (
   298	                title
   299	              )}
   300	            </h1>
   301	            <p className="sl-tagline">
   302	              {instructions.tagline ? renderInlineMarks(instructions.tagline, "sl-tagline") : subtitle}
   303	            </p>
   304	            <div className="sl-cta">
   305	              {primaryAction ?? (
   306	                <button type="button" className="sl-start" onClick={goToSections}>
   307	                  Choose a section
   308	                </button>
   309	              )}
   310	              {briefing && (
   311	                <button type="button" className="sl-brief" onClick={openBriefing}>
   312	                  Read the full instructions
   313	                </button>
   314	              )}
   315	            </div>
   316	          </motion.div>
   317	
   318	          <div className="rd-hero-space" aria-hidden="true" />
   319	        </div>
   320	      </section>
   321	
   322	      {sectionsFirst && (
   323	        <motion.section
   324	          className="rd-slab"
   325	          id="sl-sections"
   326	          ref={sectionsRef}
   327	          tabIndex={-1}
   328	          aria-label="Choose a section"
   329	          {...reveal}
   330	        >
   331	          {children}
   332	        </motion.section>
   333	      )}
   334	
   335	
   336	
   337	      {(notice || strip) && (
   338	        <div className="rd-band">
   339	          {notice}
   340	          {strip}
   341	        </div>
   342	      )}
   343	
   344	      {/* -------------------------------------------------------------- method */}
   345	      {beats.length > 0 && (
   346	        <motion.section className="rd-method" aria-label="Read, Recall, Colour steps" {...reveal}>
   347	          <div
   348	            className="sl-beats"
   349	            role="tablist"
   350	            aria-label="The method, step by step"
   351	            onKeyDown={onBeatKey}
   352	            style={{ ["--sl-beat-count" as string]: String(beats.length) }}
   353	          >
   354	            {beats.map((step, i) => (
   355	              <button
   356	                key={i}
   357	                ref={(el) => {
   358	                  beatRefs.current[i] = el;
   359	                }}
   360	                type="button"
   361	                role="tab"
   362	                id={`sl-beat-tab-${i}`}
   363	                aria-controls="sl-beat-panel"
   364	                aria-selected={pane === "beat" && beat === i}
   365	                tabIndex={beat === i ? 0 : -1}
   366	                className="sl-beat rd-step"
   367	                data-kind={STEP_KINDS[i] ?? "read"}
   368	                onClick={() => {
   369	                  setBeat(i);
   370	                  setPane("beat");
   371	                }}
   372	              >
   373	                <StepScene kind={STEP_KINDS[i] ?? "read"} />
   374	                <span className="rd-step-n">Step {i + 1}</span>
   375	                <span className="sl-l">{renderInlineMarks(step.label, `sl-bl-${i}`)}</span>
   376	                <span className="sl-d">{renderInlineMarks(step.body, `sl-bd-${i}`)}</span>
   377	              </button>
   378	            ))}
   379	          </div>
   380	
   381	          {briefing && (
   382	            <div className="sl-drawer" ref={drawerRef}>
   383	              <div className="sl-dr-tabs" role="tablist" aria-label="Instruction detail" onKeyDown={onPaneKey}>
   384	                <button
   385	                  ref={(el) => {
   386	                    paneRefs.current[0] = el;
   387	                  }}
   388	                  type="button"
   389	                  role="tab"
   390	                  id="sl-pane-tab-beat"
   391	                  aria-selected={pane === "beat"}
   392	                  aria-controls="sl-beat-panel"
   393	                  tabIndex={pane === "beat" ? 0 : -1}
   394	                  className="sl-dr-tab"
   395	                  onClick={() => setPane("beat")}
   396	                >
   397	                  This step
   398	                </button>
   399	                <button
   400	                  ref={(el) => {
   401	                    paneRefs.current[1] = el;
   402	                  }}
   403	                  type="button"
   404	                  role="tab"
   405	                  id="sl-pane-tab-briefing"
   406	                  aria-selected={pane === "briefing"}
   407	                  aria-controls="sl-beat-panel"
   408	                  tabIndex={pane === "briefing" ? 0 : -1}
   409	                  className="sl-dr-tab"
   410	                  onClick={() => setPane("briefing")}
   411	                >
   412	                  Full instructions
   413	                </button>
   414	              </div>
   415	
   416	              <div
   417	                className="sl-dr-view"
   418	                id="sl-beat-panel"
   419	                role="tabpanel"
   420	                tabIndex={-1}
   421	                data-pane={pane}
   422	                aria-labelledby={pane === "beat" ? `sl-pane-tab-beat sl-beat-tab-${beat}` : "sl-pane-tab-briefing"}
   423	              >
   424	                {pane === "beat" && current ? (
   425	                  <div className="rd-dr-step">
   426	                    <span className="sl-label">
   427	                      Step {beat + 1} of {beats.length}
   428	                    </span>
   429	                    <h3 className="sl-dr-title">{renderInlineMarks(current.label, `sl-dt-${beat}`)}</h3>
   430	                    <p className="sl-dr-body">{renderInlineMarks(current.body, `sl-db-${beat}`)}</p>
   431	                    <div className="rd-dr-nav">
   432	                      {beat > 0 && (
   433	                        <button type="button" className="sl-close" onClick={() => setBeat(beat - 1)}>
   434	                          Previous step
   435	                        </button>
   436	                      )}
   437	                      {beat < beats.length - 1 ? (
   438	                        <button type="button" className="sl-close" onClick={() => setBeat(beat + 1)}>
   439	                          Next step
   440	                          <ArrowRight size={13} strokeWidth={2.4} aria-hidden="true" />
   441	                        </button>
   442	                      ) : (
   443	                        <button type="button" className="sl-close" onClick={goToSections}>
   444	                          Choose a section
   445	                          <ArrowDown size={13} strokeWidth={2.4} aria-hidden="true" />
   446	                        </button>
   447	                      )}
   448	                    </div>
   449	                  </div>
   450	                ) : (
   451	                  <div className="sl-essay">
   452	                    {briefing.lede && <p className="rd-lede">{renderInlineMarks(briefing.lede, "sl-lede")}</p>}
   453	                    {briefing.sections.map((section, i) => (
   454	                      <section key={i}>
   455	                        <h4>{renderInlineMarks(section.heading, `sl-h-${i}`)}</h4>
   456	                        {section.body.map((para, j) => (
   457	                          <p key={j}>{renderInlineMarks(para, `sl-p-${i}-${j}`)}</p>
   458	                        ))}
   459	                        {section.steps && (
   460	                          <ol>
   461	                            {section.steps.map((step, j) => (
   462	                              <li key={j}>
   463	                                <strong>{renderInlineMarks(step.label, `sl-sl-${i}-${j}`)}</strong>{" "}
   464	                                {renderInlineMarks(step.body, `sl-sb-${i}-${j}`)}
   465	                              </li>
   466	                            ))}
   467	                          </ol>
   468	                        )}
   469	                        {section.demo && (
   470	                          <ol>
   471	                            {section.demo.beats.map((b, j) => (
   472	                              <li key={j}>
   473	                                <strong>{b.kind}</strong> {renderInlineMarks(b.text, `sl-demo-${i}-${j}`)}
   474	                              </li>
   475	                            ))}
   476	                          </ol>
   477	                        )}
   478	                      </section>
   479	                    ))}
   480	                  </div>
   481	                )}
   482	                {pane === "briefing" && (
   483	                  <div className="sl-dr-side">
   484	                    <button type="button" className="sl-close" onClick={() => setPane("beat")}>
   485	                      Back to the steps
   486	                    </button>
   487	                    <button type="button" className="sl-close" onClick={goToSections}>
   488	                      Choose a section
   489	                      <ArrowDown size={13} strokeWidth={2.4} aria-hidden="true" />
   490	                    </button>
   491	                  </div>
   492	                )}
   493	              </div>
   494	            </div>
   495	          )}
   496	        </motion.section>
   497	      )}
   498	
   499	      {/* ------------------------------------------------------- what counts */}
   500	      <motion.section className="rd-counts" aria-labelledby="rd-counts-title" {...reveal}>
   501	        <div className="rd-counts-head">
   502	          <h2 id="rd-counts-title" className="rd-h2">
   503	            Your own words, <em>every</em> detail.
   504	          </h2>
   505	          <p className="rd-method-sub">
   506	            Three ways to say the same stimulus back. Only one of them is the drill.
   507	          </p>
   508	        </div>
   509	        <div className="rd-counts-grid">
   510	          <div className="rd-count" data-tone="good">
   511	            <span className="rd-count-tag">Retelling</span>
   512	            <p className="rd-count-quote">
   513	              “The planners want to widen the road, but the last three cities that tried it got a fifth
   514	              more traffic in two years, so it will probably backfire.”
   515	            </p>
   516	            <p className="rd-count-note">Own words. Every detail. Held together as an argument. This is the drill.</p>
   517	          </div>
   518	          <div className="rd-count">
   519	            <span className="rd-count-tag">Reciting</span>
   520	            <p className="rd-count-quote">
   521	              “City planners argue that widening Elm Street will ease congestion. But in the three cities
   522	              that widened comparable roads…”
   523	            </p>
   524	            <p className="rd-count-note">Word for word. Coverage looks perfect and nothing was understood.</p>
   525	          </div>
   526	          <div className="rd-count">
   527	            <span className="rd-count-tag">Listing</span>
   528	            <p className="rd-count-quote">“Elm Street. Planners. Three cities. A fifth. Two years. Worse.”</p>
   529	            <p className="rd-count-note">Every fact, no argument. Nothing holds it together.</p>
   530	          </div>
   531	        </div>
   532	      </motion.section>
   533	
   534	      {/* --------------------------------------------------------------- slab */}
   535	      {!sectionsFirst && (
   536	        <motion.section
   537	          className="rd-slab"
   538	          id="sl-sections"
   539	          ref={sectionsRef}
   540	          tabIndex={-1}
   541	          aria-label="Choose a section"
   542	          {...reveal}
   543	        >
   544	          {children}
   545	        </motion.section>
   546	      )}
   547	
   548	
   549	      {/* -------------------------------------------------------------- close */}
   550	      <section className="rd-close" aria-label="Start">
   551	        <div className="rd-close-panel">
   552	          <p className="rd-close-line">
   553	            Twenty-five minutes. No going back. <em>Every detail you can still hold.</em>
   554	          </p>
   555	          {closingAction ?? (
   556	            <button type="button" className="sl-start" onClick={goToSections}>
   557	              Choose a section
   558	              <ArrowDown size={16} strokeWidth={2.4} aria-hidden="true" />
   559	            </button>
   560	          )}
   561	        </div>
   562	      </section>
   563	    </div>
   564	  );
   565	}

export function SectionLibraryLaunch({
  title,
  subtitle,
  instructions,
  children,
  onExit,
}: SectionLibraryLaunchProps) {
  return (
    <SectionLaunchFrame
      title={title}
      subtitle={subtitle}
      instructions={instructions}
      onExit={onExit}
      showBreadcrumbs={false}
      sectionsFirst
    >
      <div className="sl-runs">
        <div>
          <span className="sl-label">Your sections</span>
          <div style={{ marginTop: 12 }}>{children}</div>
        </div>

        <aside className="sl-aside">
          <span className="sl-label">Before you start</span>
          <MethodSummary />
          <p>
            Your entire section is recorded in <strong>one take</strong>. You
            can pause: the text hides and the clock stops while you are away,
            and you cannot go back to a stimulus.
          </p>
          <p>
            Every completed attempt stays here. If you run a section again, it
            is saved as a new attempt. Your earlier attempts are never replaced.
          </p>
        </aside>
      </div>
    </SectionLaunchFrame>
  );
}
=== SectionLaunch 110,165 ===
    [history],
  );
  const stats = useMemo(
    () => statsFor(runs, timeGoalMinutes ?? null),
    [runs, timeGoalMinutes],
  );

  return (
    <SectionLaunchFrame
      title={title}
      subtitle={subtitle}
      instructions={instructions}
      onExit={onExit}
      meta={
        <>
          {sourceSection ? `${sourceSection} · ` : ""}
          {typeof itemCount === "number" ? `${itemCount} stimuli` : ""}
          {timeGoalMinutes ? ` · est ${timeGoalMinutes} min` : ""}
        </>
      }
      primaryAction={
        <button type="button" className="sl-start" onClick={onStart}>
          Start the section
          <ArrowRight size={16} strokeWidth={2.4} aria-hidden="true" />
        </button>
      }
      closingAction={
        <button type="button" className="sl-start" onClick={onStart}>
          Start now
          <ArrowRight size={16} strokeWidth={2.4} aria-hidden="true" />
        </button>
      }
      /* Warned, not refused: Joshua's call, 2026-09-04. She is told what the
         run needs and left free to make the call herself, which is why nothing
         here disables Start. The run itself carries an on-screen way to advance
         so that going ahead is a real choice rather than a trap. */
      notice={
        noKeyboard ? (
          <p className="sl-notice" role="status">
            This one is built for a laptop. It records you for the whole section
            in one take, so a call or a locked screen ends the run, and the
            microphone on a phone hears you less well. You can carry on if you
            want to.
          </p>
        ) : null
      }
      strip={showHistory ? <StatStrip state={history} stats={stats} /> : null}
    >
      {showHistory && <RunHistory state={history} />}
    </SectionLaunchFrame>
  );
}

=== 1,140 ===
/* The Read, Recall, Colour door.
 *
 * One evening, in two registers. The hero is the runner's own indigo lifted
 * into a dusk sky over a painted meadow, cut into depth planes that move at
 * their own rates as she scrolls; everything under it sits on cream paper in
 * the same light. The method is performed before it is explained, the four
 * steps are the next thing she meets, and the sections she can run sit
 * directly beneath.
 *
 * Every value is scoped to `.sl`. Nothing here touches the runner
 * (`recall/section.css`) or the shell outside this door. Class names beginning
 * `sl-` are the door's contract with its tests and its two wrappers; `rd-`
 * names are the design and may change freely.
 */

.sl {
  /* The hero panel's ink. Named so a rule that borrows it onto cream is
     visible as a mistake instead of reading as an ordinary colour. */
  --rd-on-dark: #fff;
  --rd-cream: #f6f3ea;
  --rd-paper: #fffdf8;
  --rd-paper-2: #f3efe4;
  --rd-ink: #16132b;
  --rd-ink-2: #46435f;
  --rd-ink-3: #6a6784;
  --rd-line: #e3ded2;
  --rd-line-2: #ede9df;
  --rd-indigo: #1a1635;
  --rd-indigo-2: #2a2450;
  --rd-lavender: #c9c3ec;
  --rd-apricot: #f3c9a0;
  --rd-apricot-light: #ffe8c8;
  --rd-marigold: #f2b544;
  --rd-marigold-ink: #6e4600;
  --rd-marigold-wash: rgba(242, 181, 68, 0.26);
  --rd-emerald: #0e6f4d;
  --rd-emerald-wash: rgba(14, 111, 77, 0.11);
  --rd-display: "Fraunces", "Iowan Old Style", "Times New Roman", serif;
  --rd-sans: "Inter", ui-sans-serif, system-ui, -apple-system, sans-serif;
  --rd-mono: "IBM Plex Mono", ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, monospace;
  --rd-ease: cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1);
  --rd-radius: 22px;
  --rd-radius-panel: 28px;

  min-height: 100%;
  background: var(--rd-cream);
  color: var(--rd-ink);
  font-family: var(--rd-sans);
  font-size: 16px;
  line-height: 1.5;
  -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
  accent-color: var(--rd-ink);
  caret-color: var(--rd-marigold-ink);
  scrollbar-color: #c9c3b6 var(--rd-cream);
}
.sl ::selection {
  background: var(--rd-marigold-wash);
  color: var(--rd-ink);
}
.sl :focus-visible {
  outline: 2px solid var(--rd-ink);
  outline-offset: 3px;
  box-shadow: none;
  border-radius: 6px;
}
.sl em {
  font-style: italic;
}

/* ---- the one micro-label style ---- */
.sl-label {
  font-family: var(--rd-mono);
  font-size: 11px;
  letter-spacing: 0.16em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--rd-ink-3);
}

/* =============================================================== hero */

.rd-hero {
  padding: 14px 14px 0;
}
.rd-hero-panel {
  position: relative;
  isolation: isolate;
  overflow: hidden;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  min-height: calc(100svh - 28px);
  border-radius: var(--rd-radius-panel);
  background: #2a2450;
  color: var(--rd-on-dark);
}

/* The scene: five planes and a stage, each its own layer. */
.mw {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  z-index: 0;
  overflow: hidden;
}
.mw-layer {
  position: absolute;
  inset: -6% 0 0 0;
  width: 100%;
  height: 106%;
  display: block;
  overflow: visible;
  will-change: transform;
}
.mw-sky {
  z-index: 1;
}
.mw-far {
  z-index: 2;
}
.mw-mid {
  z-index: 3;
}
.mw-near {
  z-index: 4;
}
.mw-grain {
  z-index: 5;
  inset: 0;
  height: 100%;
  opacity: 0.13;
  mix-blend-mode: soft-light;
  pointer-events: none;
}
.mw-stage {
  position: absolute;
  left: 0;
  right: 0;
  bottom: 11%;
  z-index: 6;
  display: flex;
  justify-content: center;
  padding: 0 22px;

.rd-sc-sweep {

.rd-sc-page-turned {
  left: 46%;
  right: -30px;
  bottom: -46px;
  transform: rotate(-7deg);
  opacity: 0.92;
}
.rd-sc-stroke {
  position: absolute;
  left: 8%;
  top: 40%;
  width: 84%;
  height: 34px;
  background: var(--rd-marigold);
  border-radius: 18px 22px 20px 16px / 30px 26px 32px 28px;
  transform: rotate(-2.5deg);
  transform-origin: left center;
  animation: rd-stroke 3.4s var(--rd-ease) infinite;
}
@keyframes rd-stroke {
  0% {
    transform: rotate(-2.5deg) scaleX(0);
  }
  45%,
  85% {
    transform: rotate(-2.5deg) scaleX(1);
    opacity: 1;
  }
  100% {
    transform: rotate(-2.5deg) scaleX(1);
    opacity: 0;
  }
}
.rd-sc-chip {
  position: absolute;
  left: 26px;
  bottom: 24px;
  padding: 10px 14px 11px;
  background: var(--rd-paper);
  border-radius: 12px 12px 4px 12px;
  font-family: var(--rd-display);
  font-style: italic;
  font-size: 15px;
  color: var(--rd-ink);
  box-shadow: 0 16px 28px -18px rgba(110, 70, 0, 0.55);
}
.rd-sc-chip-head {
  display: block;
  margin-bottom: 2px;
  font-family: var(--rd-mono);
  font-style: normal;
  font-size: 10px;
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--rd-marigold-ink);
}
.rd-sc-page-fading .rd-sc-line {
  animation: rd-fade 3.6s ease-in-out infinite;
}
.rd-sc-page-fading .rd-sc-line:nth-child(2) {
  animation-delay: 0.15s;
}
.rd-sc-page-fading .rd-sc-line:nth-child(3) {
  animation-delay: 0.3s;
}
.rd-sc-page-fading .rd-sc-line:nth-child(4) {
  animation-delay: 0.45s;
}
@keyframes rd-fade {
  0%,
  30% {
    opacity: 1;
    filter: blur(0);
  }
  55%,
  85% {
    opacity: 0;
    filter: blur(4px);
  }
  100% {
    opacity: 1;
    filter: blur(0);

     1	import fs from "node:fs";
     2	import path from "node:path";
     3	import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
     4	
     5	const CSS = fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, "section-launch.css"), "utf8");
     6	
     7	/**
     8	 * A `var(--name)` whose custom property is never defined does not fail loudly.
     9	 * It falls back, or it inherits, and the result is text nobody can read that no
    10	 * test notices. `--rd-ink-soft` did exactly that here: two rules asked for it,
    11	 * it had never been declared, and the section headings quietly rendered fainter
    12	 * than the body copy underneath them.
    13	 *
    14	 * CLAUDE.md carries the same warning from an earlier round, where three token
    15	 * names shipped undefined for five months and removed the focus ring from every
    16	 * shared Button.
    17	 */
    18	describe("section-launch.css", () => {
    19	  const defined = new Set([...CSS.matchAll(/(--rd-[a-z0-9-]+)\s*:/g)].map((m) => m[1]));
    20	  const used = [...CSS.matchAll(/var\((--rd-[a-z0-9-]+)/g)].map((m) => m[1]);
    21	
    22	  it("defines every --rd- token it uses", () => {
    23	    const missing = [...new Set(used)].filter((n) => !defined.has(n));
    24	    expect(missing).toEqual([]);
    25	  });
    26	
    27	  it("gives the two buttons their own focus ring, not the world's", () => {
    28	    // `.sl :focus-visible` sets one ring for the whole world and loses the
    29	    // cascade on these two classes, so Start and Back matched :focus-visible
    30	    // and computed outline-style: none. Measured in the browser, not inferred.
    31	    expect(CSS).toContain(".sl .sl-start:focus-visible");
    32	    expect(CSS).toContain(".sl .sl-brief:focus-visible");
    33	    // The door draws both on a dark panel, where an ink ring is invisible.
    34	    expect(CSS).toContain(".rd-hero .sl-start:focus-visible");
    35	  });
    36	
    37	  it("draws no text in flat white on the briefing page, which sits on cream", () => {
    38	    // The hero classes are white for the door's dark panel. Any of them reused
    39	    // on .sl-briefing-page must restate a colour.
    40	    const scoped = [...CSS.matchAll(/\.sl-briefing-page\s+\.([a-z0-9-]+)\s*\{([^}]*)\}/g)];
    41	    for (const [, cls, body] of scoped) {
    42	      expect(`${cls}: ${body}`).not.toMatch(new RegExp(String.raw`color:\s*(#fff\b|var\(--rd-on-dark\))`, "i"));
    43	    }
    44	  });
    45	});

     1	/**
     2	 * A preview harness for the section launch surface. Development only.
     3	 *
     4	 * It exists so the component can be looked at in a real browser, in every state
     5	 * and at every breakpoint, without signing in and without a graded section
     6	 * sitting in the database. Only the network is stubbed: `window.fetch` is
     7	 * replaced before anything imports the Supabase client, so the component, the
     8	 * client helper and the derivation all run exactly as they do in the product.
     9	 *
    10	 * Not routed and not imported by the app. Reached at
    11	 * `/src/dev/section-launch-preview.html` on the dev server. It is not an entry
    12	 * point in the Vite build, which only builds `index.html`, so it never ships.
    13	 *
    14	 * States: ?state=runs|oneRun|empty|failed|noHistory|library  ·  ?theme=light|dark
    15	 */
    16	import { createRoot } from "react-dom/client";
    17	// The section JSONs deliberately ship an empty instructions block -- the rules

/* Motion is a courtesy, never the mechanism. Every state above is reachable
   with this on; continuous loops stop rather than speed up. */
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .sl *,
  .sl *::before,
  .sl *::after {
    animation: none !important;
    transition: none !important;
  }
  .mw-layer,
  .mw-stage,
  .rd-hero-copy {
    will-change: auto;
  }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------- the briefing page
   The instructions on their own page, before anything records. One column,
   everything open, Start at the top and again at the foot. */
.sl-briefing-page {
  max-width: 880px;
=== 595,630 ===
.rd-wave i:nth-child(2n) {
  height: 8px;
}
.rd-wave i:nth-child(4) {
  height: 11px;
}
.rd-wave[data-live] i {
  animation: rd-wave 0.85s ease-in-out infinite alternate;
}
@keyframes rd-wave {
  from {
    height: 3px;
  }
  to {
    height: 12px;
  }
}
.rd-caret {
  display: inline-block;
  width: 2px;
  height: 1em;
  margin-left: 1px;
  vertical-align: -2px;
  background: var(--rd-apricot-light);
  animation: rd-caret 1s steps(2) infinite;
}
@keyframes rd-caret {
  50% {
    opacity: 0;
  }
}
.rd-colour {
  align-self: flex-end;
  max-width: 90%;
  background: var(--rd-marigold);
  color: var(--rd-ink);
=== 1005,1030 ===
  right: 24px;
  bottom: 24px;
  padding: 6px 15px;
  font-family: var(--rd-mono);
  font-size: 11px;
  letter-spacing: 0.06em;
  color: var(--rd-ink-2);
  background: var(--rd-on-dark);
  border-radius: 8px;
  box-shadow:
    0 3px 0 #cfc9bd,
    0 0 0 1px #e3ded2,
    0 12px 20px -12px rgba(22, 19, 43, 0.5);
  animation: rd-press 3.6s ease-in-out infinite;
}
@keyframes rd-press {

Each edit must match exactly once, or nothing is written.\nimport fs from \"node:fs\";\nconst edits = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(process.argv[2], \"utf8\"));\nconst byFile = new Map();\nfor (const e of edits) {\n  if (!byFile.has(e.file)) byFile.set(e.file, fs.readFileSync(e.file, \"utf8\"));\n  let src = byFile.get(e.file);\n  const n = src.split(e.old).length - 1;\n  if (n !== 1) {\n    console.error(`FAIL ${e.file} :: ${n} matches for:\\n${e.old.slice(0, 120)}`);\n    process.exit(1);\n  }\n  byFile.set(e.file, src.replace(e.old, e.new));\n}\nfor (const [f, src] of byFile) fs.writeFileSync(f, src);\nconsole.log(`ok: ${edits.length} edits across ${byFile.size} files`);\nPATCHER\necho written", "description": "Write an exact-match patcher"}

{"command": "SP=\"C:/Users/Joshu/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/C--Users-Joshu-dev-ai-os/fffa8ec5-cd71-482e-a459-d36142834305/scratchpad\" && cat > \"$SP/patch.mjs\" <<'PATCHER'\n/**\n * Applies a marker-delimited edit list. Every OLD must match exactly once or\n * nothing is written, so a stale needle fails loudly instead of half-patching.\n *\n *   @@FILE <path>\n *   @@OLD\n *   ...text...\n *   @@NEW\n *   ...text...\n *   @@END\n */\nimport fs from \"node:fs\";\nconst text = fs.readFileSync(process.argv[2], \"utf8\").replace(/\\r\\n/g, \"\\n\");\nconst blocks = text.split(\"@@FILE \").slice(1);\nconst byFile = new Map();\nlet count = 0;\nfor (const block of blocks) {\n  const nl = block.indexOf(\"\\n\");\n  const file = block.slice(0, nl).trim();\n  const body = block.slice(nl + 1);\n  const oldStart = body.indexOf(\"@@OLD\\n\") + 6;\n  const newStart = body.indexOf(\"\\n@@NEW\\n\");\n  const endStart = body.indexOf(\"\\n@@END\");\n  const oldText = body.slice(oldStart, newStart);\n  const newText = body.slice(newStart + 7, endStart);\n  if (!byFile.has(file)) byFile.set(file, fs.readFileSync(file, \"utf8\").replace(/\\r\\n/g, \"\\n\"));\n  const src = byFile.get(file);\n  const n = src.split(oldText).length - 1;\n  if (n !== 1) {\n    console.error(`FAIL ${file}: ${n} matches for >>>${oldText.slice(0, 140)}<<<`);\n    process.exit(1);\n  }\n  byFile.set(file, src.replace(oldText, newText));\n  count++;\n}\nfor (const [f, src] of byFile) fs.writeFileSync(f, src);\nconsole.log(`ok: ${count} edits across ${byFile.size} files`);\nPATCHER\necho ok", "description": "Write the marker-delimited patcher"}

Six groups skewed about the ground line read the same at a\n * glance -- skew, not rotation, because it leans the tips and leaves every\n * base planted -- and cost the browser six. {"action": "javascript_exec", "tabId": "seed", "text": "\n// The pane scales CSS pixels, so sizes come from getComputedStyle (declared px)\n// and targets are proved by hit-testing rather than by reading a rect width.\nconst scale = (() => { const e=document.querySelector('.sl'); return +(e.getBoundingClientRect().width / e.offsetWidth).toFixed(4); })();\n\nfunction target(el, wantW, wantH) {\n  const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();\n  const cx = r.left + r.width/2, cy = r.top + r.height/2;\n  const hw = (wantW/2 - 0.5) * scale, hh = (wantH/2 - 0.5) * scale;\n  const pts = [[cx-hw,cy-hh],[cx+hw,cy-hh],[cx-hw,cy+hh],[cx+hw,cy+hh],[cx,cy]];\n  const hits = pts.map(([x,y]) => { const t=document.elementFromPoint(x,y); return t===el || el.contains(t); });\n  return { wants:`${wantW}x${wantH}`, cornersHit: hits.filter(Boolean).length + '/5', pass: hits.every(Boolean) };\n}\n\nconst play = document.querySelector('.rd-demo-play');\nconst crumb = document.querySelector('.sl-crumb-back');\nconst tabs = [...document.querySelectorAll('.sl-dr-tab')];\nconst focusables=[...document.querySelectorAll('a[href],button,input,select,textarea,[tabindex]:not([tabindex=\"-1\"])')].filter(e=>e.getClientRects().length);\n\n// focus ring actually used on the demo button\nplay.focus();\nconst ring = getComputedStyle(play).outlineColor;\nplay.blur();\n\nreturn {\n  scale,\n  animationsAtRest: document.getAnimations().length,\n  bladeGroups: document.querySelectorAll('.mw-blades > g').length,\n  bladePaths: document.querySelectorAll('.mw-blades path').length,\n  grainWillChange: getComputedStyle(document.querySelector('.mw-grain')).willChange,\n  ariaLiveInDemo: document.querySelectorAll('.rd-demo [aria-live]').length,\n  firstFourTabStops: focusables.slice(0,4).map(e=>`${e.className.split(' ')[0]}: ${(e.getAttribute('aria-label')||e.textContent||'').trim().slice(0,28)}`),\n  targets: {\n    play: target(play, 44, 44),\n    crumb: crumb ? target(crumb, 44, 44) : 'missing',\n    drawerTabHeights: tabs.map(t=>getComputedStyle(t).height),\n  },\n  demoFocusRing: ring,\n  headings: [...document.querySelectorAll('.sl h1,.sl h2,.sl h3,.sl h4')].map(h=>`${h.tagName} ${h.textContent.trim().slice(0,36)}`),\n};\n"}

{"action": "javascript_exec", "tabId": "seed", "text": "\nfunction targetProbe(sel, wantW, wantH) {\n  const el = document.querySelector(sel); if (!el) return {sel, missing:true};\n  el.scrollIntoView({block:'center'});\n  const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();\n  const cx=r.left+r.width/2, cy=r.top+r.height/2;\n  const hw=wantW/2-1, hh=wantH/2-1;                 // 1px inside the wanted target\n  const pts=[[0,0],[-hw,0],[hw,0],[0,-hh],[0,hh],[-hw,-hh],[hw,-hh],[-hw,hh],[hw,hh]];\n  const hits=pts.map(([dx,dy])=>{const t=document.elementFromPoint(cx+dx,cy+dy); return t===el||el.contains(t);});\n  // widen until it stops hitting, to report the real target box\n  let w=0; while(w<80 && document.elementFromPoint(cx+w/2, cy)===el) w+=2;\n  let h=0; while(h<80 && document.elementFromPoint(cx, cy+h/2)===el) h+=2;\n  return {sel, visual:`${Math.round(r.width)}x${Math.round(r.height)}`, wants:`${wantW}x${wantH}`,\n          allNinePointsHit: hits.every(Boolean), measuredTarget:`${w}x${h}`};\n}\nconst out = {\n  viewport: `${innerWidth}x${innerHeight}`,\n  play: targetProbe('.rd-demo-play', 44, 44),\n  crumb: targetProbe('.sl-crumb-back', 44, 44),\n  drawerTabs: [...document.querySelectorAll('.sl-dr-tab')].map(t=>getComputedStyle(t).height + ' x ' + getComputedStyle(t).width),\n};\nwindow.scrollTo(0,0);\nawait new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,400));\nout.animationsAtRestTop = document.getAnimations().length;\n// scroll the hero away and confirm the meadow + demo actually stop\nwindow.scrollTo(0, innerHeight*2.2);\nawait new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,700));\nout.heroOffScreen = {\n  mwStill: document.querySelector('.mw').hasAttribute('data-still'),\n  demoStill: document.querySelector('.rd-demo').hasAttribute('data-still'),\n  runningAnimations: document.getAnimations().filter(a=>a.playState==='running').length,\n  pausedAnimations: document.getAnimations().filter(a=>a.playState==='paused').length,\n};\nwindow.scrollTo(0,0);\nreturn out;\n"}

     5	/**
     6	 * One truth about pausing.
     7	 *
     8	 * For a while the runner shipped a working Pause button while six separate
     9	 * student-facing statements told her the section could not be paused. She
    10	 * either believed the copy and sat through a knock at the door, or she pressed
    11	 * the button and learned that the writing on this drill is not to be trusted.
    12	 * Joshua's call, 2026-09-09: Pause stays, the copy tells the truth.
    13	 *
    14	 * The truth is one sentence, and every surface says the same one: the text
    15	 * hides, the clock stops, and she cannot go back to a stimulus. If the button
    16	 * is ever removed, delete this file in the same commit rather than around it.
    17	 */
    18	const HERE = __dirname;
    19	
    20	/** Every place a student reads about the shape of the run. */
    21	const SURFACES = [
    22	  "components/SectionLaunchFrame.tsx",
    23	  "components/SectionLaunch.tsx",
    24	  "components/SectionLibraryLaunch.tsx",
    25	  "components/SectionBriefingPage.tsx",
    26	  "recall/coach.ts",
    27	  "content/read-recall-colour-section.instructions.json",
    28	];
    29	
    30	/** Claims that were true before the Pause button and are lies after it. */
    31	const DENIALS = [
    32	  /no pause/i,
    33	  /cannot pause/i,
    34	  /can't pause/i,
    35	  /never pauses?/i,
    36	  /does not pause/i,
    37	  /no stopping/i,
    38	  /no breaks/i,
    39	];
    40	
    41	/**
    42	 * Comments carry the reasoning and are allowed to discuss a pause; only what
    43	 * renders is under test. This strips `//`, `/* *\/` and `{/* *\/}` so a
    44	 * design note cannot fail the build and a lie cannot hide inside one either.
    45	 */
    46	function rendered(source: string): string {
    47	  return source.replace(/\/\*[\s\S]*?\*\//g, " ").replace(/^\s*\/\/.*$/gm, " ");

    49	
    50	describe("what the drill says about pausing", () => {
    51	  for (const rel of SURFACES) {

None\n * of that is visible in a screenshot and none of it was under test, so each\n * one is pinned here.\n */\nconst instructions = instructionsJson as unknown as ActivityInstructions;\n\nfunction renderDoor(props: Partial<Parameters<typeof SectionLaunchFrame>[0]> = {}) {\n  return render(\n    <SectionLaunchFrame\n      title=\"Read, Recall, Colour · PT105 Section 2\"\n      subtitle=\"Say it back.\"\n      instructions={instructions}\n      onExit={() => {}}\n      {...props}\n    >\n      <div>the sections</div>\n    </SectionLaunchFrame>,\n  );\n}\n\nconst LEVEL = (h: Element) => Number(h.tagName[1]);\n\ndescribe(\"the door's outline, focus order and live regions\", () => {\n  it(\"starts at h1 and never skips a level on the way down\", () => {\n    const { container } = renderDoor();\n    const levels = [...container.querySelectorAll(\"h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6\")].map(LEVEL);\n\n    expect(levels[0]).toBe(1);\n    for (let i = 1; i < levels.length; i++) {\n      expect(levels[i] - levels[i - 1]).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1);\n    }\n    // Every section down the page owns a heading, not just an aria-label.\n    expect(levels.filter((l) => l === 2).length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3);\n  });\n\n  it(\"keeps the outline intact with the full instructions open\", async () => {\n    const { container } = renderDoor();\n    screen.getByRole(\"tab\", { name: /full instructions/i }).click();\n\n    const levels = [...container.querySelectorAll(\"h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6\")].map(LEVEL);\n    for (let i = 1; i < levels.length; i++) {\n      expect(levels[i] - levels[i - 1]).toBeLessThanOrEqual(1);\n    }\n  });\n\n  it(\"never announces the demonstration, which turns over every few seconds\", () => {\n    const { container } = renderDoor();\n    expect(container.querySelectorAll(\".rd-demo [aria-live]\")).toHaveLength(0);\n  });\n\n  it(\"puts the way out before the demonstration's Play button\", () => {\n    const { container } = renderDoor();\n    const focusable = [...container.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(\"a[href],button,[tabindex]:not([tabindex='-1'])\")];\n\n    expect(focusable[0]).toHaveClass(\"sl-crumb-back\");\n    const play = focusable.findIndex((el) => el.classList.contains(\"rd-demo-play\"));\n    const exit = focusable.findIndex((el) => el.classList.contains(\"sl-crumb-back\"));\n    expect(play).toBeGreaterThan(exit);\n  });\n\n  it(\"holds every loop still until its surface is on screen\", () => {\n    // The IntersectionObserver stub in the test setup never reports a crossing,\n    // which is exactly the off-screen case: nothing should be running.\n    const { container } = renderDoor();\n    expect(container.querySelector(\".mw\")).toHaveAttribute(\"data-still\");\n    expect(container.querySelector(\".rd-demo\")).toHaveAttribute(\"data-still\");\n    expect(container.querySelector(\".rd-method\")).toHaveAttribute(\"data-still\");\n  });\n});\nTEST\nnpx vitest run src/features/activity-centre/components/SectionLaunchFrame.a11y.test.tsx --reporter=basic 2>&1 | tail -30", "description": "Add and run the door a11y test", "timeout": 300000}

104:- Touch targets are 44x44px, and meet it by being that size rather than by a side effect such as wrapped text. This one is a house standard above the floor, not part of it: 44x44 is WCAG 2.5.5, which is Level AAA. The binding target-size rule is 2.5.8 at 24x24, so a control that clears 24x24 on a fine pointer clears the floor and is not a defect.

## user

Tool result:
**WCAG 2.1 AA is a binding floor across every surface.** It is a defect gate, not an aspiration. A finding below the AA bar is a bug and is fixed, not argued.

- Text contrast is at least 4.5:1 against its actual rendered background, and at least 3:1 for large text and for the visual boundaries of interactive controls. Students read dense argument text on this platform for hours at a time.
- Every interactive element has a visible focus indicator.
- `prefers-reduced-motion` is respected. Motion is never load-bearing for understanding or for completing a task.
- Touch targets are 44x44px, and meet it by being that size rather than by a side effect such as wrapped text. This one is a house standard above the floor, not part of it: 44x44 is WCAG 2.5.5, which is Level AAA. The binding target-size rule is 2.5.8 at 24x24, so a control that clears 24x24 on a fine pointer clears the floor and is not a defect.
- Screen readers are informed of state that changes without a page load: unread counts, send confirmations, and live updates.
- Contrast is verified against what actually renders in the browser, not against the token values a theme is assumed to apply. The theme layer has shipped a light card on a near-black page while the token file read as correct.

{"command": "SP=\"C:/Users/Joshu/AppData/Local/Temp/claude/C--Users-Joshu-dev-ai-os/fffa8ec5-cd71-482e-a459-d36142834305/scratchpad\" && cat > \"$SP/product.patch\" <<'PATCH'\n@@FILE C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu/PRODUCT.md\n@@OLD\n- Screen readers are informed of state that changes without a page load: unread counts, send confirmations, and live updates.\n@@NEW\n- A dense row of marks that exists to be read at a glance is the one place the 44x44 house standard yields. The Report Card's ledger is 22 to 26 ticks inside an 820px card; at 44px wide they would need 968 to 1144px, so meeting the standard means scrolling or wrapping, and the ledger stops being a single picture of the run. They are 44 tall and 28 to 30 wide, they clear 2.5.8, and that is the settled answer rather than an open defect. Joshua's call, 2026-09-09.\n- Where a control has to stay small because it sits in chrome set in 11 or 13px type, the 44x44 target may be an explicit transparent overlay centred on the control instead of the control's own box. An overlay is a target somebody designed and it is proved by hit-testing the four edges; wrapped text is an accident and still does not count. The runner's rail controls, the door's crumb out and the demonstration's Play button are all built this way.\n- Screen readers are informed of state that changes without a page load: unread counts, send confirmations, and live updates.\n@@END\nPATCH\nnode \"$SP/patch.mjs\" \"$SP/product.patch\"", "description": "Record the two target-size carve-outs in PRODUCT.md"}
